--- Commit Message ---
LU-19921 tests: add GSSIAM security test

Add 301-310 for GSSIAM security flavor in sanity-sec
security test, including dyn_nm_helper() fileset cleanup
and test_300 rootsquash check updates.

Add 180a-e in recovery-small and 204, 205 in
replay-single.sh for GSSIAM.

Add OBD_FAIL_SEC_GSSIAM_MOCK_TEST and
OBD_FAIL_SEC_GSSIAM_EXP_CLIENT fail_loc tunables for
testing GSSIAM in local node environments.

Add -e option to l_gssiam_upcall and update
l_gssiam_upcall(8) man page.  Add MOCK_GSSIAM_RW,
MOCK_GSSIAM_RO, MOCK_GSSIAM_EXPIRED, and
MOCK_GSSIAM_DENY mock principal handling in
l_gssiam_auth.c.

Update sptlrpc_rule_set_choose() loopback selection
in sec_config.c and exclude gssiam in
flvr_cnt_cli2mdt/cli2ost/mdt2mdt/mdt2ost helpers.

Signed-off-by: Di Wang <ddiwang@google.com>
Change-Id: Ic2d797ca7c49ac3df06773f96915b563dcd6c0d8

--- Message (Gerrit AI review for Lustre, 2026-08-22 10:33:13.000000000) ---
Patch Set 10:

(3 comments)

AI code review results below, please doublecheck

Mostly good, minor comments inline.

One coverage gap worth closing: the mock-principal rejection added to tgt_gssiam_verify() is the only change here that alters the shipped MDT/OST authentication path, and nothing exercises it. Every new subtest calls gssiam_setup(), which sets fail_loc=0x1205 on all server nodes and thereby disables the rejection, so the branch is never taken in any of 301-310, 180a-e or 204/205. A multi-node case that leaves the fail_loc unset, sets the gssiam rule and expects the mount to fail would cover the branch that a production cluster actually runs.

Some concerns, mostly carried over from the previous round.

On patchset 19 Sebastien asked whether this should be part of the patch that introduces the code, and Andreas agreed: neither the feature landing without its tests nor this patch being left behind afterwards is wanted. That is still open here. Changes 64320, 65064, 66739 and 64321 are all still unlanded, so the ordering problem has not gone away.

It also has more weight now than it did at patchset 19, because this revision is no longer only test scripts. It changes shipped behaviour in lustre/target/tgt_gssiam.c, lustre/ptlrpc/sec_config.c and lustre/ptlrpc/gss/sec_gssiam.c, and adds new constants to include/uapi/linux/lustre/lustre_user.h. Moving those hunks down into the feature patches whose behaviour they change would let them be reviewed against the code they belong to, and would leave a suite-only change here that can be squashed or landed with the series.
